                                                                                1. 現状デザインのまま、赤味だけ落とす




                The DOWA Group has established a unique recycling-oriented
                business model by combining five businesses, and the transi-
                tion to a circular economy is a major tailwind for the Group.
                                                                                   中期計画 2024 の振り返り
                                                                                  （経済的価値、社会的価値）
                Driven by our recycling-oriented business model based on
                the technologies we have developed over 140 years, we will
                pursue high-quality cycles with the aim of achieving true,
                integrated, long-term circularity.

                By continuing to pursue quality in circularity,
                we will contribute to the realization of
                a sustainable society and further enhance
                our corporate value.

    DOWA’s Origin




      Our Ideas and Technology                                                                                  From “Black Ore” with Many Impurities,
                                                                                                                We Extracted Many Different Kinds of
      Came from Limited Resources                                                                               Metal and Even More Knowledge

      and a Disadvantageous Location.                                                              Separation         Technology
                                                                                                 DOWA began as a mining and smelting company in Kosaka,
                                                                                                 Akita Prefecture. The mine’s silver ore resources were depleted
                                                                                                 a little over a decade after our founding, but we succeeded in
      Because “black ore” has so many impurities, our extraction and smelting technology         developing smelting technology for the large quantity of “black
      were created.                                                                              ore” (complex sulfide ore) that lay untapped underneath.
                                                                                                 Through our experience in separating and recovering a variety
      Because our location made it difficult to import raw materials, we transitioned to 100%    of metals from “black ore” with many impurities, we developed
      recycled materials at our smelters.                                                        separation technology that has been an immense strength in
                                                                                                 our later recycling work.
      Because this was uncharted territory, we took on the challenge of building a global net-
      work for the recovery of recyclable resources.

      It was by pursuing methods for further utilizing these resources under these limitations
      that we arrived upon the ideas and technologies that now distinguish DOWA.                   Collection         System
                                                                                                 Once it became the norm to import ore from overseas, it
                                                                                                 became difficult to procure enough ore in Kosaka, an inland
                                                                                                 region of Akita Prefecture. To survive, we repurposed the site
                                                                                                 into a smelter of recycled resources, ahead of the world, and
                                                                                                 expanded our recovery of these resources. This enabled us to
                                                                                                 quickly build a global network for resource recovery and estab-
                                                                                                 lish a collection system for a variety of resources.




                                                                                                   Continuity         Is Our Duty
                                                                                                 As mine operators, we are responsible for the livelihoods of the
                                                                                                 people who work there. We ensure that people have what they
                                                                                                 need in order to live, as well as developing municipal infrastruc-
                                                                                                 ture and taking the optimal environmental measures. Through
                                                                                                 corporate management based on founder FUJITA Denzaburo’s
                                                                                                 philosophy of “coexistence with workers and the residents of
                                                                                                 local communities,” we were working to uphold our duty for
                                                                                                 better continuity of communities and the environment before
                                                                                                 the term “sustainability” entered the public lexicon.

    Overall Picture of the DOWA Group


    Facts and Figures                                                                                                                                          Business Overview

                                                                                                                                                                                   Environmental Management & Recycling Business
                                                                                                                                                                                   Total handling of waste, from collection to incineration/landfilling and recycling, ahead of the industry.
                                                                                                                                                                                   With comprehensive strength in waste handling and accurate analysis technology, we have built a reli-
                                                                                                                                                                                   able network to recover as many resources as possible from waste. Waste that cannot be repurposed is
                                                                                                                                                                                   incinerated and landfilled to reduce environmental impact.

                                                         · Embrace change and turn it into an opportunity to
                                                                                                                                                       DOWA’s founder, Fujita Denzaburo, was a businessman during Japan’s Meiji era who contributed
                                                            challenge ourselves                                                                        to the industrial world through his involvement in a broad range of businesses that included con-
                                                         · Respect diversity and individuals                                                           struction, railways, textile spinning, power generation, and newspapers, in addition to mining.
                                                                                                                                                             Fujita founded the Company in Kosaka Town (Akita Prefecture), where he donated large sums
                                                                                                                                                       to elementary schools based on the conviction that education is the source of national wealth
                                                                                                                                                       and power. Further, through the Company’s predecessor, Fujita Gumi, he established social capi-
                                                            The exemplary behavior and judgment for
                      Code                                 the officers and employees of the DOWA Group
                                                                                                                                       Founder

                                                                                                                                     FUJITA
                                                                                                                                                       tal by introducing electric utilities, constructing railways, maintaining waterworks, and building
                                                                                                                                                       schools, housing, hospitals, and leisure facilities, such as theaters and sports centers.
                                                                                                                                    Denzaburo
                                                                                                                                     (1841–1912)
                                                                                                                                                             This social capital was made available not only to those directly or indirectly involved with the
                                                                                                                                                       mine but also to residents of local communities. Sustainability management at the DOWA Group
                                                                                                                                                       is corporate management conducted from a long-term perspective, drawing from Fujita
                                                                                                                                                       Denzaburo’s philosophy of “coexistence between workers and the residents of local
                                                                                                                                                       communities.”

     DOWA’s Value Creation




     DOWA’s History
                                                                                                                                                   Since its founding in 1884, the DOWA Group has worked in the field of basic materials to support the economic development of
                                                                                                                                                   Japan. Over the course of its history of more than 140 years, the Group has transformed its business and improved and developed its
                                                                                                                                                   corporate structure while being subject to the variety of business conditions that come with the changing times. Therefore, it is safe to
                                                                                                                                                   say that our ability to adapt to change, developed over many years, is a characteristic of the DOWA Group.

     DOWA’s Strengths

          Recycling-Oriented Business Model                                                                                  DOWA’s Strengths: The Source of Our Recycling-Oriented Business Model


                                                                                                                                                 Collect
             The DOWA Group has a unique recycling-oriented business model that combines its five core
             businesses.
               We offer a wide range of products and services, from materials to parts, with a wide range of
             applications. These products and services are developed by leveraging our unique and advanced
             technological capabilities, earning us top market shares and high profitability. In addition, by provid-
                                                                                                                                                 Collection of Recyclable Resources
             ing products and services to a wide range of markets, we have created a highly diverse and stable
             profit structure that is less susceptible to trends in any one market.                                          Global Collection Network
                                                                                                                             Our Company has established a global recyclable resource collection network spanning Japan, Asia, North
                                                                                                                             America, and Europe. With pre-processing functions such as crushing, sorting, and heat treatment, as well as
                                                                                                                             both dry and wet smelting methods, we are able to handle a wide variety of recyclable resources—this is one of
                                                                                                                             our key strengths. Additionally, we have established a high level of reliability in sampling and analytical preci-
                                                                                                                             sion. Through direct sales, flexible customer service, and accumulated know-how in material collection, we meet
                                                                                                                             the diverse needs of our clients.

     ates solely on recyclable resources without the need for ore-based raw materials (Kosaka Smelting & Refining                                             technologies, such as single-crystal and thin-film growth technology for semiconductor materials, technology to

     Group technologies form the foundation of the complex.

     Medium-term Plan




         Review of Materiality
     We reviewed our materiality at the start of Medium-term Plan 2027. An issue with our previous materiality was that the
     direction and scope of specific activities were difficult to understand. To rectify this, we redefined our goal for 2030 for
     each materiality item and formulated a roadmap to accomplish each goal. Our materiality in Medium-term Plan 2027
     was set based on this.

     Creating a Resource-Recycling Society: Enhancing Products and                                                                   Services that Reduce Social and Environmental Risks

     The global transition to a circular economy has accelerated in recent years. The DOWA Group considers material recy-
     cling to be its raison d’être. Through our unique recycling-oriented business model, we aim to make effective use of
                                                                                                                                         Initiatives Under Medium-term Plan 2024
     limited resources to contribute to the realization of a circular economy.                                                           Our Group has built a recycling-oriented business model that realizes a cycle from the collection of recycled raw materi-
        Today, with recycling becoming increasingly widespread, we aim to achieve a more integrated, long-term, true circu-              als to the efficient production of metals and the enhancement of their value.
     larity. By employing new ideas and the technology we have developed thus far while coordinating with industry and
     society as a whole, we pursue quality in circularity.

     Responding to Climate Change                                                                                                                                                                                   For more information on our Climate Change Policy, please see the link below.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                    https://hd.dowa.co.jp/en/csr/esg/policy/biodiversity.html




     Climate change is a serious social challenge facing all industries and people around the world. By addressing climate                                           Strategies 
     change through both the reduction of our GHG emissions and the creation and expansion of products and services that
     contribute to society’s efforts to combat climate change, we aim to achieve a carbon-neutral society by 2050, leading                                           To identify risks and opportunities that climate change could pose for the Group’s business, we deliberated based on
     to the sustainable growth of the DOWA Group.                                                                                                                    multiple scenarios from 1.5°C to 4°C, identified risks and opportunities related to climate change in short (by 2025),
                                                                                                                                                                     medium (by 2030), and long (by 2050) time frames, and summarized our analysis and evaluation in a TCFD report that
       For more information on our Climate Change Policy, please see the link below.                                                                                 we published in 2022. In fiscal 2024, with the establishment of Medium-term Plan 2027, we updated our short- and
       https://hd.dowa.co.jp/en/csr/esg/policy/biodiversity.html                                                                                                     medium-term outlooks, created common prediction scenarios for the whole of the Company, and organized current issues
                                                                                                                                                                     in each business, after which we reviewed our risks and opportunities and established a three-year execution plan.

     Responding to Climate Change




        GHG Emissions Reduction Targets                                                                                                                                       Initiatives Under Medium-term Plan 2027

     Protecting the Environment                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     For more information on protecting the environment, please see the link below.

     The DOWA Group recognizes the impact of its business activities on the environment. We are working throughout the                                                                                                 Initiatives under Medium-term Plan 2024
     entire Group to implement initiatives for the environment and society through our core businesses and by positioning a
     reduction in the environmental impact of our business activities as an important issue for management.                                                                                                            Under Medium-term Plan 2024, we carried out various activities, including interviews on progress toward providing
                                                                                                                                                                                                                       support for the building of management systems, commencing operation of workflow systems for incident reports, and
                                                                                                                                                                                                                       conducting location risk surveys for natural disasters at domestic offices.

     The DOWA Group engages in making its risk management more sophisticated to prevent situations that could have a major                       Initiatives Under Medium-term Plan 2024
     impact on its business and to prevent or mitigate damage in the unlikely event such a situation comes to pass. In addition,
     we will continue efforts to enhance and perfect our risk management workflow, which involves identifying apparent and                       Under Medium-term Plan 2024, we established elements required for internal governance as follows and carried out
     potential risks at each operating company, implementing countermeasures, reviewing them, and conducting audits.                             measures to enhance each of these elements.


                                                                                                                                                   Elements Required for Internal Governance
     System for Risk Management                                                                                                                    1. Governance Environment	Corporate governance regarding matters such as corporate culture, management policies, and
                                                                                                                                                                              organizational systems
     The Group has established a risk management system based on the Four Lines Model for internal control, modeled on                             2. Risk Assessment and Response      Identification of and response to risks that will impede us from achieving organizational targets
     three lines of defense. The operating subsidiaries responsible for production and other operations (first line) and the                       3. Governance Activities             Activities to mitigate identified risks
     operating companies that oversee them (second line) develop a risk management cycle that includes periodic risk                               4. Information and Communication     Building of processes to provide, share, and acquire information needed by organizations
     assessments. In contrast, each DOWA Holdings division (third line), as the holding company, provides necessary                                5. Monitoring                        Supervision (by management and committees) and third-party audits
     instructions, supervision, and support. Furthermore, the Internal Audit Department of DOWA Holdings (fourth line)
     ­conducts internal audits to evaluate the effectiveness of these measures.

     3. Governance Activities                                                                                                      Quality Management
                                                                                                                                   We place quality at the core of our management, and are committed to improving the quality of our products and
     Definition of DOWA Standard Processes                                                                                        ­services at our major manufacturing plants, which have obtained quality management systems certifications such as
                                                                                                                                    ISO 9001. We also focus on ensuring the quality of procured items and work with our suppliers to guarantee the quality
     We established DOWA Standard Processes to document appropriate operational procedures and judgment standards for               of raw materials and auxiliary materials through regular investigations and audits.
     handling various apparent and potential risks in our business operations, and have begun activities to standardize and
     clarify our governance activities. As the Group’s activities span a wide range of operations, we balance the effect of cre-
     ating definitions with operational workload as we carry out our activities.
                                                                                                                                   Initiatives Under Medium-term Plan 2024

     4. Information and Communication                                                                                              Under Medium-term Plan 2024, we carried out various measures with the aim of building a quality assurance frame-
                                                                                                                                   work at or above industry standards. Through these measures, we exceeded the targets set for the indicators “Creation
                                                                                                                                   of an ISO 9001 compliant system” and “Establishment of quality assurance education.”

     Remuneration for Directors and Audit & Supervisory Board Members                                                                                        Reduction of Cross-shareholdings 
     The remuneration system for directors comprises basic remuneration, which is a fixed amount; performance-based                                           The Company positions its cross-shareholdings as those that will enhance the Company’s corporate value to maintain
     remuneration, which considers the Group’s consolidated performance; and restricted stock remuneration. This system                                       and strengthen relationships with business partners and other parties and form a strong relationship of trust with the
     does not apply to outside directors, however, as they take on a supervisory role from an independent and objective per-                                  issuing company. Regarding cross-shareholdings, the Board of Directors regularly makes a comprehensive judgment
     spective. Therefore, they are ineligible for any remuneration based on individual performance. As each auditor is inde-                                  as to whether or not to continue to hold stocks. If it is judged that continuing to hold a stock will not contribute to
     pendent from the execution of business operations, only fixed remuneration is paid to each auditor. The amount of                                        the improvement of the Company’s corporate value, the stock is successively sold, taking into account impacts on
     remuneration is determined through discussions among the auditors within the total amount of remuneration approved                                       the market.
     at the General Meeting of Shareholders.                                                                                                                     In fiscal 2024, the Company sold a portion of one listed stock held by the Company. Additionally, on December 10,
                                                                                                                                                              2024, the Board of Directors evaluated whether to continue holding cross-shareholdings. As a result, for fiscal 2024,
                                                                                                                                                              apart from the aforementioned stock, the decision was made to continue holding the remaining stocks.

        Remuneration Determination Process                                                                                                                    Support Framework for Outside Directors/ Outside Auditors 
            Calculation of the portion linked to the absolute amount of ordinary income
           · The standard amount is calculated by multiplying the rate of increase or decrease in the actual ordinary income against the standard ordinary   Our four outside directors gather information through means such as attending critical meetings, reading necessary
              income amount, which is predetermined in alignment with the Medium-term Plan.                                                                   materials, and meeting with other directors and employees. Our part-time outside auditors (two of our three outside
            Calculation of the portion linked to the ordinary income target achievement rate
                                                                                                                                                              auditors) visit the Company for two or three days of each month, attending meetings with the other auditor to facilitate
           · The standard amount is calculated by multiplying the target achievement rate, derived by dividing the actual ordinary income by the target
              amount (the announced ordinary income forecast), by a fixed amount.                                                                             sharing of information. The part-time auditors are included in on-site inspections and consultations of our business
           · Taking into account potential extreme fluctuations in metal prices, exchange rates, global pandemics, and other unforeseen social and eco-      sites, subsidiaries, etc.
              nomic changes, the target achievement rate is capped at a range of 50% to 150%.
            Determination of remuneration amount
           · The standard amount for each position is calculated by applying the position-based payment rate to the sum of the standard amounts from
              and .
           · The final remuneration amount is determined by multiplying the position-based standard amount by individual performance.




        Restricted Stock Remuneration
        Restricted stock remuneration shall be provided by granting monetary remuneration claims to eligible directors based on a Board of
        Directors’ resolution. The directors will then pay these claims to the Company as a contribution in kind to issue (or dispose of) the
        Company’s common stock with restrictions on the transfer of these shares until retirement or resignation. The amount of the monetary
        remuneration claims is determined based on the director’s position, and the per-share price is set at the closing price of the Company’s
        stock on the Tokyo Stock Exchange on the business day preceding the date of the Board of Directors, resolution regarding the issuance
        (or disposal) of shares. This system aims to provide the directors with incentive to sustainably enhance the Company’s corporate value
        while promoting greater value-sharing with shareholders.

     The DOWA Group recognizes that it is essential to promote data utilization through the use of digital technologies,                                    Examples of Improvements through DX 
     including artificial intelligence (AI) and the Internet of Things (IoT), to transform manufacturing, the foundation of the
     DOWA Group’s business, and to evolve its operations and business activities. Based on this recognition, we have estab-                                 As a result of the increase in personnel with knowledge of DX in the Group, we were able to carry out full-scale DX at
     lished a DX Basic Policy called Manufacturing Transformation (DOWA Transformation). We will promote DX by building                                     various Group companies.
     IT infrastructure, strengthening information security, and developing DX-proficient human resources.                                                     In the three-year period from fiscal 2022, we made over 100 improvements to sites through the use of AI.
